# KTM 250 XC-F 2025: aftermarket parts

4680 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 451 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 646 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3583 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
